[EVENT: SYR116287]
Date: 2023-08-19
Country: Syria | Location: Kafra
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Kafra Communal Militia (Syria) [Identity militia]
Actor2: Kafra Communal Militia (Syria) [Identity militia]
Fatalities: 3
Sources: Liveuamap; SHAAM; SOHR [Local partner-New media]

DESCRIPTION:
On 19 August 2023, armed clashes between two families took place in Kafra in Aleppo province over the Kafra town Council's leadership in the community, killing two individual and injuring three others including the current head of the Local Council. On 21 August, the head of the Local Council succumbed to wounds sustained during the clashes. The victims were brothers. 3 fatalities.

[EVENT: SYR116382]
Date: 2023-08-19
Country: Syria | Location: Sidnaya Military Prison
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Syria (2000-2024) Prison Guards [State forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Syria) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 2
Sources: SOHR [Other]

DESCRIPTION:
Around 19 August 2023 (month of), a young man and an elderly man who are relatives from Atman died under torture in Sidnaya Military Prison in rural Damascus. The young man was arrested despite signing a reconciliation agreement with the regime in 2018. 2 fatalities.

[EVENT: SYR116824]
Date: 2023-08-19
Country: Syria | Location: Nasib Border Crossing
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Jordan (1999-) [External/Other forces]
Actor2: Smugglers (Syria) [Political militia]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Dar'a 24; SY24 [Subnational-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 19 August 2023, Jordanian anti-narcotics military members in coordination with the Jordanian anti-narcotics department fired artillery at smugglers who attempted to cross the Syrian-Jordanian border in rural Dara coded to Nasib Border Crossing, injuring several, and leading to the seizure of large quantities of Captagon pills and Hashish. There were no fatalities.
